- `CV_ENGINE_MODEL` (default `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`) · `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` — unset = labeled heuristic scoring (`is_simulated`), never a silent fake.
- `ADZUNA_APP_ID` / `ADZUNA_APP_KEY` — broader discovery beyond ATS boards. Unset = dormant.
- `CV_ADMIN_SECRET` — session cookie signing (falls back to `JWT_SECRET`).
- `CV_ADMIN_PW_<SLUG>` — **legacy bootstrap only**, read once for the four pre-existing profiles when their password hash is empty. New profiles use invites; do not add one per person.

## ENRUTA — gestión documental vehicular (carpeta `enruta/`, montado en `/enruta`)

Recordatorios de vencimiento de documentos de tránsito para el Centro de Diagnóstico Automotor del Valle (CDAV, Cali), con **Laura** como asistente. Router Express propio, Sequelize propio sobre `DATABASE_URL`, tablas `enruta_*` multi-tenant.

**EL ESTADO DE UN DOCUMENTO SE DERIVA, NUNCA SE LEE DE LA COLUMNA.** `enruta_documentos.estado` se calculaba una vez al dar de alta el documento y nada volvía a tocarlo: medio año después el tablero anunciaba 234 "por vencer" mientras `/documentos/por-vencer` devolvía lista vacía, porque 83 filas marcadas `por_vencer_30_dias` llevaban meses vencidas. `src/utils/estado.js` lo deriva de `fecha_vencimiento` en cada lectura (getter del modelo + `sqlEstado()` para agregaciones) y traduce los filtros a rangos de fecha con `whereEstado()`. Las fechas se resuelven en zona Colombia (`fecha_vencimiento` es DATEONLY; comparar contra un timestamp del servidor recorta hoy). `renovado` y `suspendido` son de ciclo de vida y se respetan tal cual. No reintroducir un `where: { estado: ... }` sobre la columna.

**LAS OPERACIONES QUE TOCAN EL ESQUEMA SON POST CON CLAVE.** Estaban como `GET /health/sync|seed|seed-test-data` sin autenticación: un rastreador corría `sync({alter:true})` sobre producción. Ahora `POST /admin/sync|seed-sedes|seed-demo` con cabecera `x-enruta-admin-key` y `{"confirmar":true}`; clave equivocada responde 404, no 403.

**Voz:** el orbe propio (`data-agent="enruta"`, voz **dalia**), no ElevenLabs. La persona vive en `src/config/voice-agents.js`; los hechos de trámites los empuja el tablero con `setContext` desde `GET /enruta/voice/laura/contexto`, que los **recorta** del prompt de Laura (`LAURA_CONOCIMIENTO`) en vez de copiarlos. Laura por teléfono no existe: el webhook contesta con `<Say>` de Polly y lo dice; el reemplazo es ConversationRelay.

**Datos de demostración:** `src/services/demo-data.js` — 80 ciudadanos del Valle con vehículos, documentos, comparendos, llamadas y renovaciones. **Las fechas son relativas al día en que se siembra** (el sembrador viejo escribía fechas literales y la demostración envejecía) y **el azar es sembrado**, así que la misma semilla da las mismas cédulas y quien prepara la demostración puede aprenderse una. Cédulas con formato colombiano real (8 dígitos pre-1988 por rango de género, 10 empezando por 1 para el NUIP). Cada fila queda marcada `[demo]` en `notas`. `node enruta/scripts/seed-demo.js [--reset]` o el endpoint.

**Dominio propio:** `enruta.digit2ai.com` (manejador de host en `src/app.js`, junto a los demás dominios propios — Express empareja en orden de registro). Mapea solo la raíz: el tablero ya pide todo con rutas absolutas `/enruta/...`. Deja pasar `/embed/`, `/api/voice-agent/` y `/api/tts/` porque el orbe deduce su origen del `src` de su propio script y reescribirle `/api/` lo dejaría mudo. **Una ruta ajena termina en un 404 de enRuta, no en el CRM.** `digit2ai.com` NO lo sirve esta app (es GoHighLevel, `sites.ludicrous.cloud` tras Cloudflare), así que `digit2ai.com/enruta` no se puede crear desde este repo: eso se hace en GHL con un marco o una redirección. Prueba: `node scripts/test-enruta-host.js` → 16/16.

**SIT:** `node enruta/sit.js` (en proceso) o `node enruta/sit.js https://aiagent.ringlypro.com/enruta` (contra el despliegue) → **63/63**, sin llaves externas, solo lectura contra producción.

**Variables de entorno:**
- `ENRUTA_ADMIN_KEY` — clave de las operaciones administrativas (cae a `JWT_SECRET`). Sin ninguna de las dos, esas rutas quedan **deshabilitadas**, nunca abiertas.
- `ENRUTA_PASSWORD` — enciende la puerta de acceso (`src/auth.js`). **Sin ella el tablero y toda la API son públicos**, con cédulas, teléfonos y comparendos. `ENRUTA_JWT_SECRET` firma la cookie (cae a `JWT_SECRET`).
- `ENRUTA_TOOLS_KEY` — autentica `/voice/*` (endpoints máquina) cuando la puerta está encendida. Si falta, `/voice/*` queda abierto y el arranque lo avisa, antes que cortar una consulta en vivo.
- `ENRUTA_SEED_DEMO=1` — siembra al arrancar. **Siembra una vez y no vuelve**: la guarda es la marca `[demo]`, así que la variable puede quedarse puesta. `ENRUTA_SEED_RESET=1` borra el tenant antes de sembrar · `ENRUTA_SEED_CLIENTES` (80) · `ENRUTA_SEED_SEMILLA` (20260901) · `ENRUTA_SEED_TENANT`.
- `ELEVENLABS_ENRUTA_AGENT_ID` — **ya no se lee en ninguna parte.**

## AI Factory Runtime — the bench as running software (folder: factory, mounted /factory)

**The 102-agent roster used to be prose**: a specialist existed only as a brief written into a subagent prompt at dispatch time, so nothing recorded that it ran, what it cost, how long it took, or whether it was any good — and the count lived in eight hand-edited files (which is how "83" survived on digit2ai.com after the bench had grown). This vertical makes the roster a runtime.

- **ONE REGISTRY:** `verticals/factory/registry/workforce.js` holds every agent (id, team, role, skills, rules, tier). Adding an agent is adding a row. `counts()` is the only place a total is computed.
- **THE DRIFT TEST IS THE ENFORCEMENT.** `sit.js` fails the build when the architect skill, the `/ai-specialist` skill, the Dispatch Board, the factory page or the digit2ai.com landing (`public/neural-intelligence.html`) quote a count, core row, SME or roster sum the registry does not produce. Change the registry, run the SIT, fix what it names.
- **Brain gateway** (`src/brain.js`): each agent registers `<id>.brief` (compiled system prompt, no model call) and `<id>.run` (executes on a task). Gates: channel (`admin|system|mcp`), role (`owner|operator` run, `reader` brief only), task size cap (20k chars), per-tenant daily cost cap. `tenant_id` from session only and DELETED from arguments. Every call, denials included, is a row in `fx_runs` with tokens, cost and latency.
- **Model tiers, not names:** `deep` (core, AI Services SMEs, vertical SMEs) = `FACTORY_MODEL_DEEP` (Sonnet 5), `fast` (specialists) = `FACTORY_MODEL_FAST` (Haiku 4.5). `src/model.js` is the ONLY file that reaches Anthropic (SIT greps). Cost is computed from the response usage against cited first-party prices; an unknown model records no cost rather than a guess.
- **EVALS ARE DETERMINISTIC AND HONEST.** `registry/evals.js` feeds agents the input their rules exist to refuse (an invoice with no printed total, a board asking for a guaranteed saving, a POC in months, a booking tool that failed) and grades with fixed checks — no model grades a model. **Keyless = `not_run`, never passed**, score null. An agent with no cases shows "no evals", not a score. 16 agents / 18 cases seeded; coverage grows by adding cases.
- **Every artifact is verified** whatever the model: empty output, emoji, and invalid JSON for JSON-only agents are flagged and recorded.
- **Keyless:** no `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` = `run` returns `executed:false, is_simulated:true` with the brief and NO artifact. Nothing is fabricated.
- **Surfaces:** dashboard `/factory/` (roster with 30-day runs, success, spend, p50 latency, latest eval; per-agent brief, run box, eval button; "Run all evals" is a background job polled by the page because a full run exceeds the ~100 s proxy ceiling) · `GET /factory/health` (open, reports config only) · `ALL /factory/mcp` JSON-RPC (`initialize`, `tools/list` = 204 tools, `tools/call`). Page in `src/views/`, never `public/`.
- **BOTH CREDENTIALS FAIL SHUT:** dashboard + API reuse `ARCHITECT_USER`/`ARCHITECT_PASSWORD` (same audience as the Dispatch Board); MCP needs `FACTORY_API_KEY`. Either unset = 503. Running agents spends money.
- **Tables:** `fx_runs`, `fx_eval_runs` (`tenant_id NOT NULL`, named indexes), created idempotently on first use; no DB = in-memory store behind the same interface (`/health` names the backend).
- **SIT:** `node verticals/factory/sit.js` → **80/80**, zero keys. NOT covered: a live Anthropic call and the Postgres store — verify with `GET /factory/health` then a run from the dashboard.

**Environment Variables:**
- `FACTORY_API_KEY` — Bearer key for `/factory/mcp`. No default; unset = MCP closed.
- `FACTORY_MODEL_DEEP` (`claude-sonnet-5`) · `FACTORY_MODEL_FAST` (`claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`) — reuse `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`.
- `FACTORY_COST_CAP_USD` (20) — per-tenant daily spend ceiling across all agent runs and evals.
- `FACTORY_TENANT_ID` (1) — tenant stamped on dashboard and MCP runs. `FACTORY_STORE=memory` forces the in-memory store.

## Architect Dispatch Board — the /ringlypro-architect reference, gated

The modes, the seven build phases, the 102-agent bench (10 core seats: 9 agents incl. the AI Specialist + the AI Readiness Department as core 10; 76 specialists; 12 AI Services SMEs; live-filterable), the routing table and the five house patterns, as one page. Internal reference, not a customer surface.

**Live:** `/architect` on the app · `architect.digit2ai.com` root once that CNAME exists · health `GET /architect/health` (open by design — it reports whether the gate is configured, never content) · debug `/debug/architect-board-error`.

**`digit2ai.com/architect` CANNOT BE CREATED FROM THIS REPO.** The apex is a GoHighLevel site (`sites.ludicrous.cloud` behind Cloudflare, currently `162.159.140.166`) and the request never reaches this app — the same wall that stopped `digit2ai.com/enruta` and `digit2ai.com/hispanotec`. That path is made in GHL as a redirect or a frame. A subdomain pointed at Render is ours, which is why `architect.digit2ai.com` is handled here instead.

**THE PAGE LIVES IN `src/views/`, NOT `public/`.** `express.static(public)` would serve it to the open internet with no credential at all — the gate would still be mounted, still pass, and the file would still be readable by anyone who guessed the filename. Keeping it outside the static root is what makes it private; the password is the second lock, not the first.

**THE CREDENTIAL HAS NO DEFAULT AND FAILS SHUT.** Either variable unset = the board answers **503**, never open. This estate has been bitten by the other choice: a console shipped with a fallback password read as configured, and anyone who had read the repo could sign in. Compares are constant-time over SHA-256 digests (so the length of the real secret does not leak through `timingSafeEqual`'s length check), and both the username and password compare always run — an early return on a bad username makes "wrong user" measurably faster than "wrong password". The username is matched case-insensitively; the password is not. Responses carry `no-store` + `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`.

An unowned path on `architect.digit2ai.com` redirects to that host's root rather than falling through — falling through serves the entire CRM on a brand subdomain, which is the `jobmd.io/admin` lesson.

**Environment Variables:**
- `ARCHITECT_USER` — the sign-in address. No default. SET on Render.
- `ARCHITECT_PASSWORD` — the password. No default. Unset (either one) = the board is CLOSED, not open.

## MaraMed — presentación narrada (ISTC × Digit2AI)

`public/maramed-presentacion.html` + `public/maramed-audio/s1..s12.mp3` + `scripts/generate-maramed-audio.js`. Doce láminas en español para MaraMed, la plataforma de monitoreo, priorización y gestión proactiva de poblaciones de salud que **ISTC (Ingeniería y Servicios Tecnológicos Colón) desarrolla y opera, con Digit2AI aportando la capa de IA** — la misma estructura de alianza que AgroMercado, y se nombra así en el mazo. Live: `https://aiagent.ringlypro.com/maramed-presentacion.html`.

**Mismo motor de mazo que `ComplianceMind_Demo_BDT.html`**: escenario fijo 1280x720 escalado a la ventana, un `<audio>` pre-renderizado por lámina, subtítulo sincronizado con la barra de progreso, avance automático al terminar la pista, teclado completo (flechas, espacio, Home/End, F, T) y reflujo a columna bajo 820 px. La primera lámina espera un gesto porque el navegador bloquea el audio automático.

**EL TEMA SE LEYÓ DE `maramed.vercel.app`, NO SE INVENTÓ.** Urbanist 300-700, `--bg-dark:#09111e`, `--medical-teal:#0d9488`, `--accent-blue:#38bdf8`, `--accent-amber:#f59e0b`, `--accent-red:#f43f5e`, más el violeta `#8b5cf6` que RayScan usa. Un mazo que no se parece al producto obliga al espectador a creer que son lo mismo.

**LAS PANTALLAS SON HTML, NO CAPTURAS.** Cinco láminas reconstruyen la aplicación en marcos de teléfono: ingreso biométrico, los cuatro estados del orbe de MARA (inactivo / escuchando / razonando / respondiendo), la historia clínica agrupada por especialidad con el color del triaje, VitalScan en sus tres momentos y el informe CAD de RayScan completo. Una captura se proyecta borrosa, no reacomoda en un móvil y envejece el día que cambia el producto.

**LA HONESTIDAD ESTÁ EN EL CONTENIDO, NO EN UN DESCARGO AL PIE.** El recuadro `LIMITACIÓN DEL SISTEMA` del informe se reproduce **entero y legible** dentro del marco — la IA puede alucinar hallazgos, el informe no es un diagnóstico válido y exige validación por un radiólogo certificado. Las dos etiquetas de cada signo vital se comentan una por una (SpO2 dice NORMAL con confianza BAJA, y eso se dice en voz alta). Y la lámina once separa **funciona hoy / es prueba de concepto / lo que NO afirmamos**: no hay cifra de sensibilidad ni de especificidad porque ese estudio no se ha corrido, y no se invoca registro sanitario alguno. No inventar una métrica clínica aquí es la regla que gobierna el mazo.

**EL GUION VIVE UNA SOLA VEZ.** `scripts/generate-maramed-audio.js` **lee el objeto `NARR` del propio HTML** en vez de repetirlo, y aborta si una lámina quedó sin narración o una pista sin lámina. Tener el texto en dos archivos garantiza que un día el subtítulo y la voz digan cosas distintas — el defecto que el espectador nota y nadie más. Voz Dalia (`es-MX-DaliaNeural`) a `-4%` por `src/services/edge-tts.js`: sin API key, cero dólares. `node scripts/generate-maramed-audio.js [--force]` → 12/12 pistas, 3,87 MB.

**El alto se ajustó contra medición, no a ojo.** Chrome mide `scrollHeight` de cada lámina y de cada `.pbody`; el marco es de 430 px salvo en las dos láminas que llevan franja inferior (`.slide.sim.tight`, 378 px), porque forzar una sola altura obligaba a recortar el contenido de las propias pantallas. Cero desbordamiento en 1280x720 y cero desbordamiento horizontal en 390 px.

**Versión MP4 para escritorio:** `node scripts/render-maramed-video.js [--mb=9.5] [--crf=12]` → **7,41 MB · 8:00 · 1920x1080 · 12 fps** (ffmpeg es dependencia **de build**, no del servidor; el MP4 no se versiona, se regenera). Tres decisiones que no conviene deshacer: (1) **la secuencia de fotogramas se arma a mano, no con el demuxer `concat`** — con directivas `duration` ffmpeg deriva su propio reloj y el video salía 717 s contra 676 s de voz, con las láminas separándose de lo que Dalia iba diciendo; ahora cada lámina ocupa `round(duración_mp3 × 12)` fotogramas y el desfase total queda en 56 ms. (2) **El bitrate se despeja del techo, no se elige**: el límite de WhatsApp es de tamaño, se reparte el presupuesto entre audio y video y se codifica en dos pasadas; subir la resolución con el mismo presupuesto empeora la imagen — a ~96 kbps, 720p se ve mejor que 1440p. (3) **El subtítulo se achica hasta que entra** (de 13,5 px hacia abajo): en video no hay barra de desplazamiento, ocho de las doce narraciones se cortaban a mitad de frase, y es justo el texto que lee quien mira WhatsApp en silencio. **El guion se apretó un 30% (10.201 → 7.181 caracteres) sin perder un solo dato ni una sola advertencia**: la primera versión duraba 11:16 y se cortaba al enviarla por WhatsApp. Como el peso es duración × bitrate, acortar el relato bajó el archivo de 11,15 a 8,56 MB **y subió** el bitrate de 96k a 120k, así que además se ve mejor. No recortar la lámina once ni el recuadro de limitación para ahorrar segundos: son la razón de ser del mazo. **EL CUERPO DEL TEXTO VA EN PESO 400, NO 300.** El mazo nació con trece declaraciones en `font-weight:300` sobre gris apagado: proyectado en sala oscura se ve elegante, en la pantalla de un portátil se ve desvaído, y **al comprimir video el trazo delgado es lo primero que se pierde** porque tiene menos contraste que defender. El peso subió a 400, la paleta de texto se aclaró (`--ink2` `#b7c4d8`→`#d3dded`, `--mut` `#8496b0`→`#a6b6cd`) y el subtítulo quemado va en blanco a peso 500, que es lo que más se lee. No volver a bajarlo a 300 por estética: el destino es una pantalla pequeña y comprimida, no un proyector.

**SE CODIFICA POR CALIDAD (CRF), NUNCA POR BITRATE.** Un mazo son doce imágenes fijas: entre lámina y lámina el video no cambia y esos cuadros no cuestan nada, así que pedir un bitrate reparte el presupuesto por igual a lo largo del tiempo y deja a los doce cuadros que importan con decenas de kilobytes cada uno. El primer corte salió **borroso** por tres causas acumuladas, todas corregidas y ninguna reversible sin volver al borrón: (a) **`-maxrate`/`-bufsize` son un techo POR CUADRO** — con bufsize del doble del bitrate cada fotograma clave quedaba limitado a ~30 KB, cuando una lámina de texto necesita 200-400 KB; (b) el `-g` dejaba **53 fotogramas clave para 12 imágenes distintas**, cuarenta y un cuadros caros que no aportaban nada y robaban bits a los otros — ahora `keyint=99999:scenecut=40` los pone sólo donde cambia la lámina; (c) se entregaba a **1280x720**, y en un monitor grande el reproductor lo amplía más de dos veces. Con CRF 12 a 1920x1080 (capturado a 3840x2160 y reducido con lanczos) el video ocupa **3,7 MB contra los 7 MB que ocupaba el 720p borroso**: pesa la mitad y se ve nítido. El script sube el CRF de dos en dos sólo si no entra en el techo, y **falla** si excede o si video y voz difieren en más de un segundo.

**Corte VERTICAL para compartir (WhatsApp):** `public/maramed-vertical.html` + `public/maramed-audio-vertical/v1..v9.mp3` + `scripts/generate-maramed-vertical-audio.js` + `scripts/render-maramed-vertical-video.js` → **1,98 MB · 56 s · 1080x1920 · 25 fps** (CRF 8; el mismo cambio a control por calidad lo bajó de 8,16 MB y lo dejó más nítido). **Es otro video, no un recorte del mazo**: once minutos en horizontal quedan como una franja ilegible en un teléfono en vertical, y nadie reenvía once minutos. Aquí las pantallas del producto —que ya son verticales— se escalan ×2,42 y ocupan el alto completo, con subtítulo quemado a 44 px porque en WhatsApp se ve en silencio. **Reutiliza el marcado de los teléfonos del mazo largo** (se extrae de `maramed-presentacion.html` en tiempo de construcción), así que las pantallas no se pueden ir separando entre las dos piezas. Nueve escenas: gancho, biometría, triaje hablado, nivel Manchester, escaneo, cinco signos, informe, **el límite del sistema** (no se omite por ser un corte comercial) y cierre ISTC × Digit2AI. Codifica **por CRF, no por bitrate**, con fotogramas clave sólo en los cambios de escena y sin `-maxrate`/`-bufsize` — ver la explicación en la versión de escritorio: es el mismo defecto y el mismo arreglo. **Falla** si excede el techo o si video y voz difieren en más de medio segundo. Cada escena lleva 0,45 s de silencio al final: sin esa cola el corte cae encima de la última sílaba.

**`maramed.app` YA NO LO SIRVE ESTE REPO.** El dominio está conectado al servicio de Render `maramed` (repo `digit2ai/maramed`, `src/public-site.js`), no al CRM: la portada, `/presentacion` (el simulador para clínicas, construido desde el cerebro con `scripts/build-presentacion.js`), el mazo y `/api/tts/edge` salen de allá. El manejador de host `maramed.app` que sigue en `src/app.js` y `public/maramed-landing.html` de este repo **no reciben tráfico del dominio**; editar la portada aquí no cambia nada en maramed.app. Aquí queda solo la demo técnica rPPG de Digit2AI en `/maramed/` (el dominio redirige `maramed.app/maramed/*` hacia aiagent.ringlypro.com). Verificado el 15-sep-2026: el 404 de marca y el 302 absoluto de `/maramed/` son del servicio `maramed`.

**Cifras admisibles:** solo las del PDF de arquitectura y de las capturas — 86 bpm, 10 rpm, 42 ms, 98 %, 126/79; luminancia 127,5, desviación 37,8, contraste 125, bordes Sobel 6,3 %; 30 s de captura rPPG; AES-128 en la trama BLE; Manchester I-V. Ninguna otra.

## BuyersLine — new-construction buyer platform (folder: incentiva, DIGIT2AI and agent partner)

**LIVES AT https://buyersline.app (owner bought the domain 2026-09-15).** A host handler in `src/app.js` (with the other brand domains) calls the vertical router directly, so the site is served at the ROOT with `baseUrl ''` (every `{{BASE}}` resolves to `/`); `www` 301s to the apex; `/embed/`, `/api/voice-agent/` and `/api/tts/` pass through to the main app (the voice orb); `/buyersline/*` and `/incentiva/*` on that host 301 to the root path; anything else ends in the vertical's 404, never the CRM. DNS: CNAME apex and www to `ringlypro-crm.onrender.com` (managed in GoHighLevel), certificate by Render. **Old address:** browser GETs to `aiagent.ringlypro.com/buyersline/*` and `/incentiva/*` 301 to `https://buyersline.app/*`; API calls, webhooks, POSTs and `/health` are not redirected, so Twilio and old cached pages keep working. `INCENTIVA_CANONICAL_HOST` (default `buyersline.app`, `off` stops the redirect). Email and SMS links default to `https://buyersline.app` (`INCENTIVA_PUBLIC_URL`).

**NAMED BuyersLine; "Incentiva" was the working name.** User-facing copy, the voice persona (`buyersline`), the monitor User-Agent (`BuyersLineMonitor`) and the mount (`/buyersline`) use the brand. Internal identifiers deliberately did NOT change: folder `verticals/incentiva`, table prefix `nca_`, env vars `INCENTIVA_*`, cookie `incentiva_token`, storage keys `incentiva_*`. `/incentiva/*` answers 301 to `/buyersline/*`. The consent version moved to `v2-2026-09-13` because the consent wording names the product. **Logo:** `public/logo-master.svg` (full-bleed square, content inside the central 80% so it is maskable) and `favicon.svg` are the single sources: a teal tile holding a white speech bubble (your direct line to an agent) with a teal roofline and a gold dot inside (a home, and the buyer). The first mark, a roofline over a rising line, is kept as `public/logo-v1-roofline.svg`. PNGs (`apple-touch-icon`, `icon-192`, `icon-512`, `favicon-32`) are rendered through Chrome, not sips. Header marks use fixed brand colors so the tile is identical in dark and light.

**Purpose:** a consumer front door for new-home buyers in Tampa Bay. A buyer sees their buying power, every active community near their target ZIP, and each builder's incentives **verified by a licensed agent**, compared in monthly-payment terms, then is connected at no cost to the agent partner, a licensed Florida sales associate who represents them. Mounted at `/buyersline` (console `/buyersline/admin`). Bilingual EN/ES, emoji-free. Founding plan with Revisions 1-2 in `docs/ventures/incentiva/INCENTIVA-FOUNDING-PLAN.md`.

**THE AGENT PARTNER IS NEVER NAMED (owner rule 2026-09-15).** The licensed agent partner currently works for a builder, so their name must not appear in any page, console, email, presentation, architecture page, SME tool, doc, comment, test, commit message or published artifact. Refer to the role only ("a licensed Florida real estate sales associate", "the agent partner"). The displayed agent name comes from `INCENTIVA_AGENT_NAME` on Render. SIT fails the build if the name appears anywhere in the vertical.

**MVP REVISION AFTER THE 2026-09-15 REVIEW (supersedes the six-stage Ana intake below where they differ).** The assistant is **Anna** (persona id still `buyersline`). Flow: **"See my options" (the only hero CTA; the buying power estimator section is gone) → Anna, 5 questions → the report renders right below the chat → contact and consent under the report → the full report is emailed.**
- **Intake (`public/intake-chat.js`, `window.BLChat`):** where (ZIP, city or neighborhood; the ONLY required answer) · max price OR max monthly (one question; an amount under $20,000 or said "a month" is monthly) · down payment · timeframe · pre-approved / cash / neither (`needs_lender`). Every other answer has Skip. The fifth answer (or skip) calls `POST /api/v1/public/searches`, which resolves the area on the server, starts or reuses the research and stores an `nca_searches` row with **no name, email or phone**. URL becomes `/?report=<token>`.
- **Report (`public/report-view.js`, `window.BLReportView`, data from `GET /api/v1/public/searches/:token`, built by `services/searchReport.js`):** wish list · purchasing power (from the max monthly: the price it buys; from a max price: its monthly; plus the yearly income needed so ALL monthly debts including the home stay under 50% of gross income, and an optional income + debts check that calls the existing `/buying-power` 40/50 formula) · today's promotions per builder with **Lennar, D.R. Horton, M/I Homes, Taylor Morrison, KB Home always listed** (`found` / `no_promotion` / `none_found` / `checking` / `not_checked`, never implying "none" when research could not run) plus any other builder found · best deal today (research ranking, else lowest estimated payment) · communities with fit (price 50, monthly 30, location 20; incentives never count) · lowest price, lowest in budget, median price per sq ft (RentCast listings within 10 miles) · school grades (only when the research's source URL was seen in that run) · homes at the price point sorted by fit · the owner's disclaimer verbatim ("Builder promotions change daily and are subject to change without notice. BuyersLine is not a real estate agent or broker.", shared constant `notify.REPORT_DISCLAIMER`) · send to your own agent by text (`sms:`) / email (`mailto:`) / copy link, or continue without an agent. **The report link is forwardable, so it carries no buyer name or contact detail** (SIT asserts). Free: no paywall or blurred unlock (SIT greps).
- **Contact form (`POST /api/v1/public/leads`, `leads.validateContact` + `createLeadFromSearch`):** first name, email, phone (all required) · "Already working with a real estate agent?" and "Already visited a new construction site?" (both required yes/no; new columns `nca_leads.visited_site`, `contact_preference`, `search_id`; `has_agent` now also accepts `yes`) · one box "Share my name, contact details and criteria with a licensed real estate agent" (ignored and hidden when they already work with an agent; they are never referred) · contact preference email or phone call · Create report. The email consent row stores the wording shown under the button (`lead-v2-2026-09-15`). One lead per search: a second submit returns the first. The buyer email (`notify.buyerLeadReport`) now carries the whole report inline. The legacy six-stage validator/`createLead` stays for the SMS double opt-in path and SIT; nothing public posts to it.
- **RESEARCH NEVER ENDS ON A FAILURE SCREEN.** The 2026-09-15 "research didn't finish" screen was the Anthropic account out of credit (`nca_research_runs.error`: "credit balance is too low"). Now `research.execute` retries 429/5xx/overloaded twice (5 s, 15 s), and a credit/401/403 error marks the model down for `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DOWN_MIN` (30) so new runs skip it. A run that cannot use the model finishes `done` with, in order: the last successful model run for the same area within `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_STALE_DAYS` (14) (`fallback_run_id`, notice `research_stale`, shown with that run's date), else registry rows, else no rows with notice `research_unavailable`. Fallback runs expire in 20 min. `POST /searches/:token/refresh` re-runs only when the current run fell back and the model is reachable. `/health` shows `research_model`.
- **THE COMPLIANCE AGENT (`engines/promoCompliance.js`) GATES EVERY PROMOTION**, when rows are stored AND on every read: holds guarantee/approval claims, fair-housing lexicon words, expired promotions, an offer with no source URL (unless agent-verified), a row with no builder; passes buydowns, lender/title requirements, missing end dates and the builder's own superlatives with notes shown beside the promotion. Stored as `nca_research_rows.compliance_status` / `compliance_notes`.
- **MORNING REFRESH:** the agents loop calls `research.dailyRefresh` every tick; between 6 and 10 a.m. Eastern it claims the date in `nca_job_runs` (one run per day across instances) and re-researches the ZIPs buyers searched in the last 30 days plus `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DAILY_ZIPS`, up to `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DAILY_MAX` (8), through the same sanitize + compliance steps. `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DAILY=off` stops it. Sources are the builders' own sites via Anthropic web search, the licensed agent's registry and RentCast. realtor.com has no public API and its terms forbid scraping, so it is NOT a source; adding aggregators means a keyed API, not a scraper.
- **VOICE LOOP FIX.** Anna repeated "Where are you looking?" because with no model the offline fallback only re-read the question and captured nothing. The orb now calls a host hook `window.D2AIVoiceOrbOffline(text)` whenever `/api/voice-agent/chat` answers `source:'heuristic'`; BuyersLine answers with `BLChat.offlineTurn`, which parses a ZIP (digits or spoken words), a city/neighborhood, amounts ("450k", "2,800 a month"), skips, timeframe and financing phrases, applies them, and returns the acknowledgement plus the next question; after the report it fills name, email ("rosa at example dot com"), phone and the two yes/no answers, never the share box. With the model up, the persona says a ZIP, city or neighborhood IS the location answer. Live context labels: `ANNA CHAT STATUS` + `REPORT ON SCREEN`. Page actions: `fill_intake_form` (five answers, contact fields, `working_with_agent` yes/no, `visited_site`, `contact_preference`, `skip`), `show_section` (top, how_it_works, report_chat, report, contact, about), `submit_intake_form` (clear yes + complete form + 5-second cancelable countdown). `select_communities` and `estimate_buying_power` were removed with the picking step and the estimator.
- **ANNA BY VOICE LISTENS LIKE A PERSON (owner voice test 2026-09-21).** Persona section HOW YOU LISTEN / CÓMO ESCUCHAS: answers in any form and order, several in one sentence, corrections replace, late answers count, never demand a format, one short clarifying guess when unclear, patient repeats and plain explanations. **Only REPORT ON SCREEN is the buyer's report**: she had quoted the landing page's featured M/I offer inside a 33543 report. When the report appears she says it is ready before asking for a name. **Place lookup (`services/area.js`) takes what the buyer said:** the typed town name wins over OpenStreetMap's admin city (Lutz was Tampa), the city's own USPS ZIP list beats OSM's postcode (Wesley Chapel was 33559 = Lutz, and the report said 'near Lutz'), roads/shops/parks count only when their name contains every word said, and a phrase of only generic words ('zip code', 'the neighborhood') is never looked up. Place rows cached before this fix were deleted from `nca_area_cache`. **Printing a report prints only the report** (`html.bl-report-on`, class-based because `:has()` is missing in older browsers; the first PDF was 34,989 pages). Best deal today requires `isRealOffer()` (research text often describes the ABSENCE of an offer). The income check says when the buyer's own monthly limit caps the price (`income_only` from `buyingPower.estimate`). Live voice test: `SCRIPT=en.json|en-flexible.json|es.json [LANG2=es] node verticals/incentiva/test/voice/anna-voice.js` (creates a search, never a lead).
- **PRIVATE-PREVIEW LOGINS (`services/archgate.js`).** The gate sign-in asks for email + password. **Create a login** (`/gate/signup`): email, password and verify password (both with a show/hide eye), 12+ characters, not a published password, bcrypt 12; the account is **pending** (maker) and cannot sign in until the owner credential approves it on `/gate/accounts` (checker; an approved login cannot open that page). The owner is emailed on each request (`INCENTIVA_SITE_APPROVER_EMAILS`, else the owner email), the person is emailed on approval. **Forgot password** (`/gate/forgot` → `/gate/reset?t=`) emails a link through the existing SendGrid sender; the token is HMAC'd with the app secret and the account's current password hash, so it works once, expires in 1 hour and needs no table. Cookie `bl_arch` = `<o|u>.<id>.<expiry>.<hmac>`: the owner's is keyed with the owner credential, an account's with its password hash, so a password change signs that person out. The INCENTIVA_ARCHITECTURE_* credential still has no default and the whole gate fails shut without it. Table `nca_site_users`. `robots.txt` and `sitemap.xml` answer through the gate.
- **SME tool needs no second password:** anyone past the gate is signed in to `/architecture/sme` automatically (owner → SME admin, approved login → SME account, created on first visit). Its own accounts and magic links still work.
- **SEO:** the landing carries `robots index, follow`, canonical `https://buyersline.app/`, hreflang en/es, Open Graph and Organization/WebSite/WebApplication JSON-LD; `/robots.txt` + `/sitemap.xml` are generated from `INCENTIVA_PUBLIC_URL`. **Google cannot index anything while the site gate is on**; set `INCENTIVA_SITE_GATE=off` at launch.
- **THE WEBSITE IS PUBLIC; ONLY THE LOG IN LINK IS SECURED (owner decision 2026-09-15, later the same day).** `archgate.siteGateOn()` is now `INCENTIVA_SITE_GATE === 'on'` (default OFF): landing, search, reports and the public API need no sign-in, so Google can index them. **One sign-in page** (`/gate/login`, email + password with show/hide, Forgot password, Create a login): the owner credential sets the gate cookie AND the owner's dashboard cookie and lands in `/admin/`; a dashboard account (`nca_users`) signs in there too and lands in `/admin/`; an approved preview login lands in `/admin/` too: **approval creates (or reactivates) an `agent`-role dashboard account with the same email and bcrypt hash** (`auth.syncFromPreviewLogin`, also run on password reset; reject/disable deactivates it; Render-managed accounts and admins are never touched), so it sees only its own leads and no Admin screens. The **Expert questionnaire** is the last left-pane link and visible to every dashboard user. `/admin/login` (the Log in link) redirects signed-in users into the dashboard and everyone else to `/gate/login?next=/admin/`; the old `login.html` shell is no longer served. Sign out clears both cookies and returns to the public home page. The architecture page still requires a sign-in either way.
- **OWNER TEXT ALERTS (owner request 2026-09-15).** When someone creates a login, every active admin with a mobile number on their dashboard account (`nca_users.phone`, editable in Users → "Text alerts" for your own row; the owner's number lives in the database, never in the repo) gets a text via `sms.staffAlert`. **Sender: the A2P-registered GoHighLevel number `INCENTIVA_ALERT_SMS_FROM` (default +18132124888), sent through the GHL Conversations API using the CRM's existing GHL connection for client `INCENTIVA_ALERT_GHL_CLIENT_ID` (default 15), read at send time because the CRM refreshes that token.** The owner's phone is a contact "BuyersLine Alerts" in that GHL location. If GHL fails, Twilio sends from the vertical's sender or `INCENTIVA_ALERT_TWILIO_FROM` (default toll-free +18886103810). Deduped per alert per admin in `nca_audit_log`; staff texts carry an email address at most, never buyer contact details. Verified live 2026-09-15: delivered from +18132124888.
- **ADMIN DASHBOARD (owner request 2026-09-15).** The landing menu no longer links Architecture; it carries **Log in** (`/admin/login`). An owner already signed in at the private-preview gate (the `INCENTIVA_ARCHITECTURE_*` credential) is taken straight into the console as the `INCENTIVA_OWNER_EMAIL` admin; approved preview logins never get a console session. Admins see an **Admin** group in the left pane: **Users** (`GET /agent/admin/users`: preview logins with Approve/Reject/Disable through `POST /agent/admin/users/preview/:id`, which emails on approval; console accounts, where an admin adds an account with a one-time temporary password, deactivates or resets it, but cannot touch their own account or the two accounts set on Render; expert questionnaire accounts), **System** (`GET /agent/admin/system`: connection checks, report/lead counts, research runs with errors, morning refresh results), **Architecture** (the gated page in a frame; a console session now opens `/architecture` too), **Expert questionnaire** (a console session signs in to the SME tool: admin to SME admin, agent to expert) and **View the website**. Every admin route is admins-only (403 for agents). A pending-login count badges Users. Header label reads "Admin dashboard" for admins.
- **Console notes and knowledge base:** notes and documents on a lead (`nca_lead_notes`, `nca_files` as Postgres BYTEA, 10 MB, type allow-list checked by magic bytes, downloads always attachment + nosniff) and a knowledge base of entries with notes, unlimited links and documents (`nca_kb_entries`, `nca_kb_links`), editable any time by the admin or the agent at `#/kb`. Migrations: `src/db.js` now runs every `migrations/*.sql` in filename order under the advisory lock (`20260915_buyersline_mvp.sql`, `20260915_console_notes_kb.sql`). Console SIT: `node verticals/incentiva/sit-console.js` → **38/38**.


**The business shape is in the code, so read it before changing billing or copy.** BuyersLine is a partnership LLC (DIGIT2AI + a licensed agent partner) and a **technology company, not a brokerage and not a lender**. The agent partner is a sales associate under his own broker and **pays the platform a flat fee per consult held**; he also co-owns it, and buyers are told so (the co-owner sentence is added to the share consent and the compensation disclosure). **There is no code path, and a DB CHECK, for any charge tied to an agreement, a registration or a closing**: a transaction-contingent fee is a referral fee only a licensed Florida brokerage may receive. SIT fails the build if one appears.

**Location:** `verticals/incentiva/` — own Express Router (`src/index.js`, `createApp()`), raw SQL through `src/db.js` on `INCENTIVA_DATABASE_URL || CRM_DATABASE_URL || DATABASE_URL` (its own instance is the goal; the partnership should be separable). **`src/db.js` executes `migrations/20260913_incentiva_tables.sql` on boot under an advisory lock**, so the migration IS the schema and cannot drift; every statement must stay idempotent. 22 `nca_` tables + one view, all `tenant_id NOT NULL`. `tenant_id` = the operating entity (`INCENTIVA_TENANT_ID`, default 1); `market_id` = the metro (`tampa-bay`).

**ONLY VERIFIED, FRESH, UNEXPIRED INCENTIVES REACH A BUYER, AND ONLY THROUGH `nca_v_incentives_buyer_safe`.** The report builder reads the view, never the version table (SIT greps it). `engines/freshness.js isBuyerSafe()` restates the rule and is re-applied when a report is READ: a pinned incentive that has since been withdrawn, superseded or expired disappears from the page and a notice says so. Expiry is evaluated in `America/New_York`. Freshness: the earlier of the builder's expiry and 21 days after verification; 10 days when no expiry is published.

**ASYMMETRIC SAFETY.** `services/monitor.js processText()` hides a visible incentive the moment it sees a decrease, a removal or narrowed terms (new lender requirement, earlier close-by), then files a card. A new or larger offer waits for the agent. Rejecting a decrease/removal card restores the hidden offer if still fresh.

**ONLY A LICENSED ACCOUNT CAN CONFIRM.** `can_verify` = role `agent` with a `license_no`. The LLC owner account (`admin`) runs the platform but gets 403 on confirm. Confirm derives `audience` from the FINAL type, so retyping a card to `broker_bonus` can never put agent compensation in the buyer view. Broker bonuses are disclosed to the buyer and never ranked.

**THE MODEL WRITES PROSE, NEVER A FIGURE.** Payments, cash to close and buying power are deterministic (`engines/payment.js`, `buyingPower.js`); cash-to-close rounds each component so the total equals the parts printed. **An unknown HOA or CDD is never zero**: the payment is marked "from". A temporary buydown always carries its Year 3+ payment. Extraction (`engines/extractor.js`, model or keyword fallback) passes `verifyExtraction()` on every path: a headline not verbatim in the source is discarded; an amount, rate or date not in the source is nulled and becomes a verifier question. The narrative (`engines/advisor.js`) is templated in both languages; a model rewrite is discarded if it adds any number or proper noun. `services/llm.js` is the only file that reaches Anthropic.

**COMPLIANCE FAILS CLOSED.** `engines/compliance.js` runs on every report in EN and ES: fair-housing lexicon (block/hold), every figure in prose must exist in the data, five required disclosures, no unverified incentive, no temporary buydown without Year 3+, no unknown fee shown as a complete payment. Block = `compliance_hold`, never releasable. Reports default to **agent approval before the buyer sees them** (`INCENTIVA_REPORT_REVIEW=auto` releases passing reports). An agent **Hold** pulls a released report back; releasing the last hold returns it to review, never straight to the buyer.

**Ranking happens before language.** Fit (`engines/fit.js`) uses property and criteria only; incentives do not add to fit. EN and ES reports list the same communities in the same order (SIT). Communities with no verified incentives are **withheld**, listed as "being confirmed", not ranked.

**Gates and consent.** A buyer under agreement with another agent is stopped and stored with no contact data. Consent rows store the SERVER's wording for that version, not client-sent text. Without `share_with_agent` the buyer has no agent of record and no agent can see them. Row ownership: `agent` sees only their buyers, reports, appointments and billing; `admin` sees the tenant.

**THE FETCH GATE EVADES NOTHING.** robots.txt groups honored (RFC 9309), 10 s per host, descriptive UA, ports 80/443 only, redirects followed by hand with every hop re-checked against private/CGNAT/IPv4-mapped addresses, two 403/429/challenge responses = `blocked`. No proxies, no CAPTCHA solving, no login walls. Builder broker emails are pasted via `POST /agent/ingest/text` (no inbound mail parse yet).

**TRANSPORTS: EMAIL ONLY IN `services/notify.js`, TEXTS ONLY IN `services/sms.js` (SIT greps every other file).** Agents compose; those two files send, and each checks consent itself. Report emails: a report waiting for approval emails the reviewer with no buyer contact details; a released report emails the buyer only with stored email consent. Agent follow-ups and meeting messages are described under the agents below. No WhatsApp transport exists. SIT deletes `SENDGRID_API_KEY` and injects a fake sender and a fake Twilio client.

**BUYER-FACING FIGURES USE EFFECTIVE SETTINGS (owner decision 2026-09-14).** Stored market settings stay NULL until a person sets them, but the buying-power calculator, `/config` and new reports read `market.effectiveSettings()`: an empty reference rate is filled from **Freddie Mac PMMS** (`services/rates.js`, public CSV, 12 h cache, last good value kept on failure, source and survey date shown on every surface), and empty tax (1.8%), insurance ($250/mo), mortgage insurance (0.5%) and closing costs (3%) from labelled Tampa Bay defaults, each basis ending "Default assumption until our agent sets a local figure". Agent-entered values always win. Only a failed rate feed with no cached value still yields "can't estimate yet". SIT sets `INCENTIVA_RATE_FEED=off` and injects the rate.

**Demo data** is fictional (`Sample Builder North`, `Sample Homes Co.`, `Sample Preserve/Lakes/Oaks`, `is_demo`), loaded only by an admin (`POST /agent/demo/seed`, `/demo/reset`) or `INCENTIVA_SEED_DEMO=1`, and every public surface shows a "Sample data" banner while it exists. The demo sets a labelled sample reference rate; real settings default to NULL so payments say "not estimated" until the agent enters a rate with its source and date.

**Endpoints:** `GET /health` · pages `/` `/r/:token` `/login` `/admin` · public `GET /api/v1/public/config` · `POST /area` · `POST /research`, `GET /research/:token` · `POST /leads`, `GET /leads/:token` · `POST /sms/inbound` · `POST /buying-power` · `POST /intake` (legacy) · `GET /reports/:token` · `POST /reports/:token/consult-request` · auth `POST /api/v1/auth/login|logout`, `GET /me` · agent `GET /today` · `GET /verifications`, `POST /verifications/:id/confirm|reject`, `POST /incentives/:id/reconfirm`, `GET|POST /incentives` · `GET|POST /builders` · `GET|POST /communities`, `PATCH /communities/:id`, `POST /communities/:id/fees|homes` · `POST /sources`, `POST /sources/:id/fetch` · `POST /ingest/text` · `GET /leads`, `GET|PATCH /leads/:id` · `GET /reports`, `GET /reports/:id/preview` (read-only, the buyer view of a held report; the console opens `/r/:token?preview=:id`, which shows an Agent preview banner and no consult form), `POST /reports/:id/approve|hold` · `GET /compliance`, `POST /compliance/:id/release` · `GET /buyers`, `GET /buyers/:id` · `POST /appointments/:id/held` · `GET /billing` · `GET|PUT /settings/market` · `POST /demo/seed|reset`. Debug: `/debug/buyersline-error`. Voice orb persona `buyersline` in `src/config/voice-agents.js`.

**(HISTORY, superseded by the 2026-09-15 revision above) THE INTAKE IS A CONVERSATION WITH ANA, NOT A FORM (2026-09-14).** The assistant was named Martha until the owner renamed her Ana the same day (display name, greetings, persona and the `ANA CHAT STATUS` live-context label; the persona id stays `buyersline`). The "Tell us what you need" form is gone. `public/intake-chat.js` (`window.BLChat`) asks one question at a time in six stages (Where · Money and timing · Promotions · About you · Two questions that protect you · How we may contact you) with a progress bar, Back, Edit and Start over. **A page refresh starts a new conversation** (owner decision 2026-09-14, after a saved conversation kept reappearing): progress lives in memory only and the old `localStorage['bl_martha_v1']` key is deleted on load; EN/ES with every Ana line in both. Editing the area or the money answers clears the research choice. The final screen is the report (criteria, the communities picked, everything found, motivated inventory, the closing "Before you visit" callout, Print); `/?lead=<token>` restores it and is the link emailed to the buyer. The old `POST /api/v1/public/intake` + agent-approved `/r/:token` report pipeline still exists but nothing on the landing calls it; a lead is not converted into an `nca_buyers` row (billing still keys on buyers and appointments).

**STAGE 03 IS A LIVE RESEARCH AGENT, AND THE CODE, NOT THE MODEL, DECIDES WHAT A BUYER SEES.** `services/research.js` runs Sonnet (`INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_MODEL`, default `claude-sonnet-5`) with Anthropic's server-side `web_search_20250305` tool on the owner's research prompt, as a **background job the page polls** (a crawl takes minutes; Cloudflare cuts requests at ~100 s), streaming `server_tool_use` / `web_search_tool_result` blocks into live progress (searches run, builders checked). One run per area is cached `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_TTL_HOURS` (24) and shared by every buyer: it is public market information. `sanitizeResearch()` then enforces: **`verified` is true only when the row's source URL is one the web search actually returned in that run** (a model saying verified is not enough; the buyer sees "Source found" or "Unverified"); a passed expiration hides the row; a fair-housing word-list block hides it; every text field is stripped of URLs; a top-deal reason is replaced with a plain one if it contains a number the row does not or trips the word list. Rows a licensed agent verified in the registry (`nca_v_incentives_buyer_safe`) join as `origin:'agent_verified'` and lead the list. **The buyer view never carries a source URL or check date** (console only), is filtered by the buyer's max price and, via the payment engine, max monthly payment, and the buyer must pick at least one community (server-enforced when rows exist). No key, past `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_MONTHLY_CAP`, or a failed run = registry-only, labelled (`notice`). **The research area is resolved on the server** (`services/area.js`: Zippopotam.us for ZIP to city, Nominatim for county and places, cached in `nca_area_cache`); place text a caller sends never reaches the prompt or the shared cache key, which would let one caller poison an area for everyone.

**LEADS AND CONSENT (`services/leads.js`).** Spec table names map to the vertical prefix: `nca_leads`, `nca_lead_visited_offices`, `nca_lead_consents`, `nca_research_runs`, `nca_research_rows`, `nca_lead_selections` (+ `nca_area_cache`). Each consent channel (email, SMS, agent referral) is its own row holding the SERVER's wording for `LEAD_CONSENT_VERSION` in the buyer's language, the timestamp, **the raw IP and user agent (consent evidence; the one place a raw IP is stored, shown only in the console)**. SMS consent without a phone is stored as not granted. A buyer under agreement with another agent keeps a first name and criteria only, every consent false, status `lost`, nobody notified. **Without agent-referral consent a lead is "Report only: no outreach consent": unassigned, no notification, and an agent cannot open it (404); the admin can.** With it, the market's default agent is assigned and notified by email (`notify.agentNewLead`, no buyer contact details) and SMS (`services/sms.js`, the ONLY Twilio file, to the agent's own `nca_users.phone` from `INCENTIVA_AGENT_PHONE`, never a buyer); the buyer's report email goes only with email consent. Reassigning in the console notifies the new agent once. **No buyer SMS is sent by anything**; `POST /api/v1/public/sms/inbound` (Twilio-signature validated) applies STOP/START/HELP, and STOP revokes that number's SMS consent.

**Console:** `#/leads` (status filter with counts, flags for under agreement / has an agent / visited sales offices / report only) and `#/leads/:id` (representation and visited offices flagged at the top, contact, criteria, picked communities and the full research snapshot with source links, check dates, verified basis and hidden rows, motivated inventory, consent records with text, version, IP and user agent, notification log, status select, admin-only assignment). Agents see only leads assigned to them.

**(HISTORY, see VOICE LOOP FIX above for the current actions) Voice: ANA IS THE ONE GUIDE FOR THE WHOLE PROCESS (owner direction 2026-09-14).** Persona `buyersline` (display name Ana, formerly Martha) is written as the platform's subject-matter expert: she knows all six stages, the buying power calculator, the result badges and what happens after the report; answers the question asked in one or two sentences and returns to the next open step; never repeats a question; declines anything unrelated to the home search. She reads `ANA CHAT STATUS` (stage, question on screen, answered, missing, optional, every community on screen with its badge and CHOSEN flag, last voice choice, contact boxes, report) plus `BUYING POWER CALCULATOR` via `D2AIVoiceOrbLiveContext`. Page actions: `fill_intake_form` (answers, `skip` for optional questions, `visited_none` / `visited_offices`), `select_communities` (by the names the buyer said; `BLChat.voiceSelect` matches only rows already on screen and chooses NOTHING when a name matches more than one, which the status reports so she asks which), `estimate_buying_power` (`window.BLEstimate` fills and runs the calculator), `show_section`, `submit_intake_form` (clear yes + last step + nothing missing + 5-second cancelable countdown). **Contact boxes remain the buyer's own taps**: no schema property can name one and SIT walks every schema. **Offline mode:** the status starts with `Fallback reply (offline mode only, ignore): [question on screen]`, and `heuristicReply` in `src/routes/voice-agent.js` speaks that line when the model is unreachable, instead of reading status text aloud. **2026-09-14 incident:** Ana "lost her training" because the Anthropic account ran out of credit (`credit balance is too low`, seen in `nca_research_runs.error`); every model-backed feature on the server degraded at once. Check that first when an assistant starts sounding like a page scraper (`degraded:true` in `/api/voice-agent/chat` responses).

**SEVEN AI AGENTS, ONE BRAIN (owner direction 2026-09-14).** Ana (guide, Haiku) · Promotions Researcher (Sonnet + web search) · Incentive Reader (Sonnet) · Report Writer (Sonnet) · **Rachel follow-up** (`services/followup.js`) · **Hand-off** (`services/handoff.js`) · **Scheduler** (`services/scheduling.js`). The architecture page is `/buyersline/architecture`: animated system map, agent roster, journey, layers, guardrails, setup checklist. Keep its facts in step with the code.
- **THE WHOLE SITE IS BEHIND THE SAME SIGN-IN UNTIL IT IS PUBLIC (owner request 2026-09-15).** A gate middleware in `src/index.js`, registered before every page, asset and API route, uses the architecture credentials (`INCENTIVA_ARCHITECTURE_USER` / `_PASSWORD`): pages answer 401 with the sign-in form (which returns the visitor to the page they asked for, through `archgate.safeNext`, so it cannot redirect off-site), APIs answer 401 JSON, and gated responses are noindex and no-store. The `bl_arch` cookie now covers `Path=/buyersline/` (the old `/architecture`-scoped cookie is cleared on every set). **Deliberately left open:** `/health`, the Twilio SMS webhook (signature-checked), the RFC 8058 one-click unsubscribe POST, and the SME tool (own accounts and emailed links). `INCENTIVA_SITE_GATE=off` opens the site on launch day; the architecture page stays gated either way. SIT sets the gate off globally and tests it on its own.
- **THE ARCHITECTURE PAGE IS SIGNED-IN ONLY AND FAILS SHUT** (`services/archgate.js`, owner request 2026-09-14). `INCENTIVA_ARCHITECTURE_USER` + `INCENTIVA_ARCHITECTURE_PASSWORD`, no defaults: either unset = 503 "closed". `GET /architecture` shows a sign-in form (401) until `POST /architecture/login` succeeds; constant-time SHA-256 compares with both always run, user case-insensitive, password case-sensitive, 10 attempts per 15 minutes per IP; 12 h HttpOnly `bl_arch` cookie scoped to `/architecture`, HMAC keyed with the app secret AND the current password (rotating the password on Render signs everyone out); `POST /architecture/logout`; `no-store` + `noindex`. **The page file lives in `src/views/architecture.html`, NOT `public/`**, so `express.static` can never serve it around the gate (SIT asserts the public file is absent). `/health` reports `architecture_page` as closed / configured / configured_weak_password (compares against the repo's published passwords).
- **ONE DIAGRAM, TWO PAGES.** `public/ecosystem-map.js` + `ecosystem-map.css` (every class `em-`, every token scoped to `.em`) render the animated map into any `[data-ecomap]`: the public landing section `#ecosystem` (right before "Questions? Ask out loud.", owner request; dark band in both themes via `main > section.eco`, built-in caption describing the tapped node, EN/ES through `EcosystemMap.setLang` called from the landing language switch) and the architecture page (`data-caption="0"`, `EcosystemMap.onSelect` opens the roster card). Edit the map there, never inline in a page. The menu bar carries **Architecture** -> `/architecture` (`nav_arch`).
- **Hand-off** runs right after every saved lead (`handoff.afterLead`, fired from `POST /leads`): readiness from `engines/readiness.js` (**fixed rules, never a model**: timing, financing, communities chosen, phone, agent status, visits; Hot >= 70, Warm >= 45, else Nurture; under agreement = `none`), an English brief with **no email or phone**, an optional suggested opening (Haiku rewording of a template, discarded by `advisor.acceptRewrite` if it adds a figure or name), then the agent email + SMS (only with referral consent), then Rachel's plan. `noResponseTick` alerts the admin and the agent once when a referred lead is still `new` after `INCENTIVA_NO_RESPONSE_HOURS` (4); the first status change away from `new` (or a meeting marked held) sets `first_response_at`.
- **Rachel** plans email touches on days 1, 3, 7, 14, 30, 60, 90 and texts on 1, 7, 30, 90 (`nca_followups`, unique per lead/kind/channel/day/change), in the buyer's language, only for channels ticked. **Consent is re-checked at SEND time**, closed/lost leads and buyers under agreement are skipped, day 14 is skipped once a consult exists, texts wait out quiet hours (before 9 am, from 8 pm Eastern). Every follow-up email carries a footer unsubscribe link (`/unsubscribe/:token` page) and RFC 8058 one-click headers (`POST /api/v1/public/unsubscribe/:token`), which revoke the consent rows and cancel queued touches. **Promotion-change alerts** compare a lead's chosen communities with the newest MODEL research run for the same area (registry-only runs are never compared; wording Jaccard < 0.6 = changed; missing = "not found in our latest search"), once per distinct change, and say the offer is unconfirmed until the agent checks it. `refreshAreas` re-runs research weekly (`INCENTIVA_FOLLOWUP_REFRESH_DAYS`, 7; uses the monthly research cap) for areas active consented leads chose in.
- **Scheduler** (rules only): the buyer books a 30-minute consult from the finished report (`#book`), only with referral consent, not under agreement, with an assigned agent; times come from `slots()` inside `nca_agent_hours` (default Mon-Fri 9-5 Eastern until the agent saves hours in Settings), at least `INCENTIVA_BOOK_MIN_HOURS` (2) ahead, within `INCENTIVA_BOOK_DAYS` (14); a partial unique index blocks double booking; one active booking per lead. Confirmation, 24 h and 2 h reminders and the post-meeting feedback request are claimed with `UPDATE ... RETURNING` before sending (multi-instance safe); booking emails are transactional, reminder texts need SMS consent. Buyer page `/meet/:token` (details, cancel, 1-5 rating); console records held / no-show and an outcome (`OUTCOMES`).
- **DOUBLE OPT-IN AND NO BUYER-TYPED TEXT IN MESSAGES (security review 2026-09-14).** The intake is public and nothing verifies the phone or email a submitter types, so without this anyone could make BuyersLine text and email a stranger for 90 days with a phishing link in the "name". Therefore: (1) a ticked SMS consent triggers exactly ONE fixed confirmation text (`sms.smsConfirmRequest`, no buyer text in it); buyer texts (`buyerSms`) require `nca_lead_consents.confirmed_at`, set only by an inbound YES/SI/START from that number, which then plans the text touches; a YES never re-grants a STOP. (2) Rachel's marketing emails require the email consent `confirmed_at`, set when the buyer opens their emailed report link (`GET /api/v1/public/leads/:token`; the token reaches a buyer only in that email); the single report email says so. Meeting emails stay transactional. (3) Buyer-typed names enter an outbound message only through `util.safeFirstName` (letters, spaces, hyphens, apostrophes, up to 40; anything else becomes an empty greeting), and the typed area text is never used, only the server-resolved city and ZIP. The console shows CONFIRMED / WAITING FOR YES REPLY / NOT CONFIRMED on each consent.
- **The loop** (`services/agents.js`, every `INCENTIVA_AGENTS_INTERVAL_MIN` = 5): refresh areas, detect promotion changes, send due follow-ups, meeting messages, no-response alerts. **It starts only when `NODE_ENV=production` or `INCENTIVA_AGENTS=on`; `INCENTIVA_AGENTS=off` stops it anywhere.** This gate exists because the local `.env` points at the production database: loading `src/index.js` from a laptop once started the loop against production (nothing was sent; only the idempotent tables were created). SIT sets `INCENTIVA_AGENTS=off`. `/health` reports `agents`.
- Console: lead list Readiness column + next consult; lead detail Hand-off (score, reasons, brief, opening), Scheduler (meetings, status, outcome), Rachel (every planned touch and why it was skipped); Settings -> Booking hours. Public API: `GET /leads/:token/slots`, `POST /leads/:token/meetings`, `GET /meetings/:token`, `POST /meetings/:token/cancel|feedback`, `POST /unsubscribe/:token`. Agent API: `PATCH /agent/meetings/:id`, `GET|PUT /agent/hours`.

**SME KNOWLEDGE CAPTURE (`/buyersline/architecture/sme`, phase 1 of the owner's brief 2026-09-15).** A signed-in, mobile-first EN/ES questionnaire where a builder-side subject matter expert answers 78 questions in 14 sections over many sessions; answers become source material for the knowledge base. Code: `src/sme/` (`auth.js`, `store.js`, `routes.js`, `questions.js` seed) + `src/views/sme.html` (outside `public/`). Tables (spec names mapped to the prefix): `nca_sme_users`, `nca_sme_auth_sessions`, `nca_sme_magic_links`, `nca_sme_sections`, `nca_sme_questions`, `nca_sme_answers`, `nca_sme_answer_versions`, `nca_sme_attachments`, `nca_sme_sessions_log`, `nca_sme_section_status`, `nca_kb`, `nca_rules`.
- **Accounts are separate from the agent console and the architecture gate.** No public signup: the bootstrap admin comes from `INCENTIVA_SME_ADMIN_EMAIL` + `INCENTIVA_SME_ADMIN_PASSWORD` (+ `INCENTIVA_SME_ADMIN_NAME`), no defaults, re-synced when those values change on Render (a password change signs that admin out); admins create SME accounts in the Accounts screen (temporary password shown once, or email a sign-in link). No admin configured and no accounts = 503 closed.
- **Auth rules (SIT asserts):** bcrypt cost 12, 12+ characters, dummy-hash compare for unknown emails; server-side sessions (random cookie token, SHA-256 stored, `bl_sme` HttpOnly SameSite=Lax Secure-in-production, path `/buyersline/architecture/sme`), 24 idle hours (`INCENTIVA_SME_IDLE_HOURS`), revoked on logout, disable or password change; CSRF token header on every mutation; login and magic-link requests check Origin and are rate limited per IP and per email; magic links are hashed, single use, 30 minutes, and requesting one never reveals whether an account exists; **opening the emailed link only shows a Continue button and the POST uses it**, so mail scanners cannot burn it. **The session query aliases its columns** (`s.id AS session_id`): selecting `u.*` next to `s.id` silently overwrote the session id with the user id and slid the wrong session's idle window (caught by SIT). Every response is noindex, no-store, no-referrer, frame-deny. `aiagent.ringlypro.com/robots.txt` is not served by this app, so noindex headers (not a robots disallow, which would hide them) keep it out of search.
- **Questionnaire:** home with progress, Continue where you left off, sections with status; one question per screen with the "why we ask" line, on-device dictation (Web Speech API, English or Spanish), autosave every 10 s and on blur, Save and next (submitted only with text), Skip for now, Back. A version row is written only when text or status actually changes; an autosave of the same text never un-submits an answer. Seeding inserts missing codes only, so admin edits to wording survive redeploys. Questions 74-75 say **Ana** (the brief said Sofia; the assistant was renamed Ana).
- **Not built yet (phase 2+):** attachments (S3), section summary + Mark complete, admin answers/versions/review/notes/export, question editor, KB pipeline (translation, `nca_kb`, rule extraction to `nca_rules`), Ana retrieval with attribution, runbook.

**ONE SEARCH WIDGET, TWO PLACES.** `public/search-widget.js` renders the form, results, map and every state for both the landing page section `#homes` (`data-mode="compact"`: 6 cards, a "See all N homes" link, no automatic request) and `/search` (`data-mode="full"`: all results, sort, URL-shareable, runs from `?zip=`). No page calls `/api/v1/public/listings` itself (SIT greps both pages). Its text follows `<html lang>`, so each page's EN/ES toggle re-renders it; on the landing its "Ask our agent" button fills the intake ZIP and scrolls there instead of reloading. Leaflet is loaded on demand when the widget nears the screen. Styles live in `site.css`.

**RentCast coverage, measured 2026-09-13 in production (5 ZIPs, 182 unique homes):** 91% sourced from Stellar MLS, coordinates on all, **no builder name or community on any listing**, the large production builders (Lennar, D.R. Horton, Pulte, Taylor Morrison) essentially absent, median prices over $1M in 3 of 5 areas, median 300 to 520 days on market, only 41 of 182 between $250K and $700K. It is a traffic and lead hook, not the inventory source; the complete feed is Stellar MLS IDX through the agent partner's broker.

**Home search + map (`/buyersline/search`, alias `/buscar`) runs on the RentCast API** (`services/rentcast.js`, `GET https://api.rentcast.io/v1/listings/sale`, header `X-Api-Key`). RentCast has NO new-construction query parameter: we request Active listings around the ZIP (limit 500, which RentCast bills as ONE request) and keep only `listingType === "New Construction"`. **THE KEY NEVER LEAVES THE SERVER; unset = the page says search is not connected, never sample houses.** One upstream request per ZIP+radius per `INCENTIVA_LISTINGS_TTL_HOURS` (24); price, bedroom and sort filters run on the cached set in `nca_listing_cache`, so a filter change costs nothing. `nca_api_usage` meters requests and an atomic claim refuses past `RENTCAST_MONTHLY_CAP` (default 45, under the free plan's 50); a cached area keeps working, labelled stale. **Builder and listing-agent phones and emails are DROPPED** before the response: a buyer who calls the sales office before being registered can lose free representation, so the card CTA sends them to intake with the ZIP prefilled (`/?zip=`). These are listings, not verified incentives, and the page says so. Map = Leaflet 1.9.4 from cdnjs + **OpenStreetMap tiles (keyless)**; CARTO basemaps now watermark "API KEY REQUIRED", so do not switch back without a key. Dark mode darkens the same tiles with a CSS filter. OSM's tile policy covers light use only: move to a keyed provider when traffic grows. If geocoding fails the search falls back to the ZIP alone and says distance search is unavailable.

**Installable PWA, two apps.** `src/services/pwa.js` generates both manifests and the worker from `req.baseUrl`; **there is no manifest on disk** (SIT fails if one appears). Buyer app: id/scope `/buyersline/`, start `/buyersline/?source=pwa`, shortcuts to Search and Report. Agent console: id/scope `/buyersline/admin/`, start `/buyersline/admin/` with the trailing slash, and the console's sign-in moved to `/admin/login` so a signed-out console never leaves its installed scope (`admin.js` redirects there; `/login` still works). Worker at `/buyersline/sw.js`: caches the shell entry by entry (never `addAll`), network-first navigations with `/offline` as the last resort, **never stores `/api/` or a buyer report (`/r/`)** — offline, a report link shows the offline page. Bump `SHELL_VERSION` when a shell file changes. `install.js` registers the worker only in a top-level window, shows an Install bar when Chrome offers it and Share → Add to Home Screen instructions on iPhone Safari (dismissal remembered 14 days; console pages opt out with `data-no-install`). **Testing trap:** Puppeteer's `setOfflineMode` does not apply to the service worker's own fetches, so it reports false passes; test offline by stopping the server.

**Mobile.** Below 820px the header nav is hidden and `nav.js` adds a 44px menu button that opens the same `.site-nav` as a panel (one nav element for desktop and phone). On the search page the map sits above the list on phones and one-finger drags scroll the page until the map is tapped. Leaflet zoom buttons are 44px on touch (the rule is `#map.leaflet-touch` because Leaflet's stylesheet loads after `site.css`). Audited at 360 and 390px: no horizontal overflow, no tap target under 44px, no text under 12px.

**Not built yet (stated, not hidden):** e-signature of the buyer agreement, builder registration packets, external calendar sync (bookings live in `nca_lead_meetings` only), WhatsApp follow-up and intake, inbound email parsing, knowledge notes for the Advisor, lead-to-buyer conversion, a custom domain.

**Visual style follows jome.com/for-builders (owner request, 2026-09-13):** Mulish for everything (Jome headlines use its own proprietary "jome" typeface, which is not used here; Mulish Black stands in), ink `#26213F`, orange `#FC4C02`, lavender-gray bands `#F4F5F9`, borders `#E7E8E9`, pill buttons. **Orange text on white is 3.5:1**, so the exact orange only fills large type (headline highlight, big figures) and 19px bold buttons; small text, links and small buttons use `#C93D00` (5.1:1). Dark theme = navy ground `#1B1730` with the same orange. Logo recolored: navy tile, white bubble, navy roof, orange dot. Own logo, copy and layout were kept; do not copy Jome's logo, imagery or wording.

**Theme: LIGHT IS THE DEFAULT** (owner switched from dark on 2026-09-13). Every page ships `<html data-theme="light">` and a head script that applies a saved `dark` before styles paint (no flash); `public/theme.js` wires the sun/moon toggle (`[data-theme-toggle]`, key `incentiva_theme`) on the landing, report, login and console. The OS preference does not override the default.

**THE HERO CARD IS A REAL BUILDER OFFER, CHECKED BY HAND (owner request 2026-09-14).** M/I Homes Tampa Bay, 2/1 buydown on a 30-year FHA loan (2.875% year 1, 4.875% years 3-30, APR 5.6236%), close by Nov 30, 2026, read on mihomes.com that day. The research agent's stored row for the same builder was WRONG (it said a 5.25% conventional rate), which is why a featured offer is read from the builder's own page and never copied from a research row. The badge says "Checked on the builder's website", not "Verified": no licensed agent confirmed it. Year 1 / Year 3+ figures are principal and interest on the builder's published example loan ($403,540) and SIT asserts they equal `payment.monthlyPI`. `REAL_EXAMPLE.closeBy` in `index.html` returns the card to the labelled fictional example after that date; replace the block with the next checked offer before then.

**Workflow circuit** (`#flow`, right after the hero; it REPLACED the three-step "How it works" section, and the nav "How it works" link points here): a deliberately dark signal band in both themes (its colors are local `--fx-*` tokens) with three lanes, You / BuyersLine AI / Licensed agent. Each of the seven steps puts its node in its lane and its card below, so the hand-offs and the human in the loop are the picture. `public/flow.js` draws the S-curve path between the nodes and, only while the band is on screen, moves a glowing signal along it and lights each step, whose card plays a small wordless demo (form filling, gauge, radar, verified stamp, Year 1/2/3+ bars, typing dots, house). **The demos carry no figures on purpose**: a marketing animation must not invent a count or a price. The HTML is fully readable with no script; reduced motion draws the path and moves nothing. On phones the circuit scrolls sideways and follows the active step. Two traps hit while building it: `main > section:nth-of-type(even)` paints the band color and outranks a bare `.flow` (the selector is `main > section.flow`), and `color-mix()` is dropped by older Chromium, so tones use `rgba(var(--tone-rgb), a)`. Labels are in the EN/ES dictionaries (`flow_*`, `flow_sub`).

**SIT:** `node verticals/incentiva/sit.js` → **145/145**, zero external keys (removes `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, geocoding off). DB sections run as tenants 990913/990914 and delete their rows. Not covered: the model extraction/narrative paths, live geocoding, live builder fetches.

**Environment Variables:**
- `INCENTIVA_OWNER_PASSWORD` — admin (LLC) console password. **No default: unset = the console is CLOSED (503).** `INCENTIVA_OWNER_EMAIL` (default `mstagg@digit2ai.com`), `INCENTIVA_OWNER_NAME`.
- `INCENTIVA_AGENT_EMAIL` / `INCENTIVA_AGENT_PASSWORD` / `INCENTIVA_AGENT_NAME` / `INCENTIVA_AGENT_LICENSE` / `INCENTIVA_AGENT_TITLE` — the licensed agent account; without a license number it cannot confirm incentives. `INCENTIVA_BROKERAGE_NAME` / `INCENTIVA_BROKERAGE_LICENSE` — shown in consent and reports. `INCENTIVA_AGENT_CO_OWNER` (default on; `0` removes the co-owner disclosure).
- `INCENTIVA_JWT_SECRET` (falls back to `JWT_SECRET`) — 12 h agent session cookie `incentiva_token`. `/health` flags a weak or published password.
- `INCENTIVA_DATABASE_URL` — own Postgres; falls back to `CRM_DATABASE_URL`/`DATABASE_URL`. `INCENTIVA_TENANT_ID` (1).
- `INCENTIVA_REPORT_REVIEW` — unset = agent approves every report; `auto` = compliance-passing reports go straight to the buyer.
- `INCENTIVA_CONSULT_FEE_USD` (350) · `INCENTIVA_CONSULT_MONTHLY_CAP` (20) — per-consult billing to the agent.
- `INCENTIVA_MONITOR_GO=1` — scheduled source fetching every 30 min (off by default). `INCENTIVA_GEOCODE=off` disables Nominatim.
- `INCENTIVA_EXTRACT_MODEL` / `INCENTIVA_ADVISOR_MODEL` (default `claude-sonnet-5`) — reuse `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; unset key = labelled keyword/template path.
- `INCENTIVA_INTAKE_PER_HOUR` (10) · `INCENTIVA_SEED_DEMO=1` (seed fictional demo data once).
- `SENDGRID_API_KEY` — enables the two emails (unset = nothing sends). `INCENTIVA_FROM_EMAIL` (falls back to `SENDGRID_FROM_EMAIL`, then `info@digit2ai.com`; must be a verified SendGrid sender) · `INCENTIVA_FROM_NAME` (`BuyersLine`) · `INCENTIVA_PUBLIC_URL` (`https://buyersline.app`, used in links, robots.txt and the sitemap) · `INCENTIVA_EMAIL=off` kill switch.
- `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_MODEL` (`claude-sonnet-5`) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_TOOL` (`web_search_20250305`) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_MAX_SEARCHES` (25 per run) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_TTL_HOURS` (24) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_MONTHLY_CAP` (150 runs, then registry-only) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_PER_HOUR` (6 fresh runs per IP; cached areas are free) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH=off`. Reuses `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`.
- `INCENTIVA_AGENT_PHONE` — the licensed agent's mobile for new-lead texts (synced to `nca_users.phone`). Unset = no text, audited `sms.agent_phone_missing`. `INCENTIVA_SMS_MESSAGING_SERVICE_SID` (A2P 10DLC, preferred) or `INCENTIVA_SMS_FROM` — the sender; reuses `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID`/`TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN`. Unset = nothing texts. `INCENTIVA_SMS_WEBHOOK_URL` — override the URL Twilio signs for the STOP webhook (default `INCENTIVA_PUBLIC_URL` + `/api/v1/public/sms/inbound`). `INCENTIVA_SMS=off`.
- `INCENTIVA_SME_ADMIN_EMAIL` / `INCENTIVA_SME_ADMIN_PASSWORD` / `INCENTIVA_SME_ADMIN_NAME` — bootstrap admin for `/architecture/sme`. No defaults; unset and no accounts = CLOSED. `INCENTIVA_SME_IDLE_HOURS` (24).
- `INCENTIVA_SITE_GATE` (OFF unless `on`) — `on` puts the whole site behind the sign-in; by default the site is public and only the dashboard and architecture page need one.
- `INCENTIVA_SITE_APPROVER_EMAILS` — comma list notified when someone creates a preview login (default: the owner email). `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DOWN_MIN` (30) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_STALE_DAYS` (14) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DAILY` (`off` stops the morning refresh) · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DAILY_ZIPS` · `INCENTIVA_RESEARCH_DAILY_MAX` (8) · `INCENTIVA_SEARCH_PER_HOUR` (30).
- `INCENTIVA_ARCHITECTURE_USER` / `INCENTIVA_ARCHITECTURE_PASSWORD` — sign-in for `/architecture` and, while the site gate is on, the whole site. No defaults: either unset = the page is CLOSED (503).
- `INCENTIVA_AGENTS` (`on` forces the follow-up/hand-off/scheduler loop outside production, `off` stops it) · `INCENTIVA_AGENTS_INTERVAL_MIN` (5) · `INCENTIVA_NO_RESPONSE_HOURS` (4) · `INCENTIVA_FOLLOWUP_REFRESH_DAYS` (7, 0 disables weekly research refresh) · `INCENTIVA_FOLLOWUP_MODEL` / `INCENTIVA_HANDOFF_MODEL` (default `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`, `off` = templates only) · `INCENTIVA_BOOK_MIN_HOURS` (2) · `INCENTIVA_BOOK_DAYS` (14).
- `INCENTIVA_RATE_FEED` (`off` disables the Freddie Mac rate) · `INCENTIVA_RATE_FEED_URL` · `INCENTIVA_DEFAULT_TAX_RATE` (0.018) · `INCENTIVA_DEFAULT_INSURANCE_MONTHLY` (250) · `INCENTIVA_DEFAULT_PMI_RATE` (0.005) · `INCENTIVA_DEFAULT_CLOSING_PCT` (3).
- `RENTCAST_API_KEY` — enables home search. Unset = search reports not connected. `RENTCAST_MONTHLY_CAP` (45) — hard stop on upstream requests per calendar month; raise it to match the plan (Foundation 1,000 / Growth 5,000 / Scale 25,000). `INCENTIVA_LISTINGS_TTL_HOURS` (24) · `INCENTIVA_LISTINGS_PER_HOUR` (60, per IP) · `RENTCAST_BASE_URL` (override for tests).

## LevelUp Media Marketing — MCP-driven AI back office for content creators (folder: verticals/levelup)

**Built 2026-09-22 from TWO source documents merged into ONE: `verticals/levelup/REQUIREMENTS.md`** (INTERNAL spec, never served). **The public `/about` page (also answering the old `/requirements` link) is `ABOUT.md`: who we are and what we do, and it NAMES NO SOURCE** (owner request 2026-09-22) — SIT greps it for the platform, community and people the sources came from. Source 1: "Creator Agent Workforce — System Architecture" (one creator's back office on a third-party agent platform, "GrokBot", from a recorded Spanish training call). Source 2: "The Integrated Content Creator System — Project Brief" prepared for Andrea (a product MANY creators sign up for). **Where they disagree the brief wins**; every such call is marked [DECISION] in the doc. Live at `aiagent.ringlypro.com/levelupmediamarketing` and, once DNS moves, `levelupmediamarketing.com`. English + Spanish, blush-pink "warm community" look, emoji-free.

**IT IS OUR OWN MULTI-TENANT ECOSYSTEM, NOT GROKBOT.** The writeup's pattern (a manager agent + single-job specialists) is kept; its platform is not (third-party credentials, single creator, vendor rules). Open self-signup; each account is its own tenant (`tenant_id = user id`). Tables `lu_` (13): users, profiles, knowledge, posts, edit_jobs, edit_issues, deals, retainers, pick_lists, pick_items, api_keys, calls. **`migrations/*.sql` runs on every boot under an advisory lock (`src/db.js`), so the migration IS the schema — keep every statement idempotent.** `LEVELUP_DATABASE_URL || CRM_DATABASE_URL || DATABASE_URL`.

**ONE MCP BRAIN IS THE ONLY DOOR TO ELEVEN AGENTS** (`src/brain.js` + `src/agents.js`, 36 tools): **Andrea** (manager and single point of contact — the tool namespace stays `lider.*`, only the NAME is Andrea, on the landing, in the dashboard and in the voice orb) · Creative Strategist · Ideas · Scripts · Calendar · Video Editor · Publisher · Business Assistant · Product Research · Top Picks · Trainer. The app calls `POST /api/v1/tools/<agent.tool>`, the creator's own AI calls `/mcp` (JSON-RPC, `lu_live_` keys, SHA-256 at rest, scopes `agent` / `train`), Andrea calls the same tools — no second path. **`tenant_id` comes from the session or the key and is DELETED from arguments.** `listTools` filters with the same `allowed()` as `callTool`. Every call, denials included, is an `lu_calls` row with no body text. Per-tenant daily cap on MODEL calls (`LEVELUP_DAILY_MODEL_CALLS`, 300), counted from the audit; past it the deterministic path runs and the audit says why.

**THE DASHBOARD IS DRIVEN BY WRITING, NOT ONLY BY BUTTONS** (`src/copilot.js`, `POST /api/v1/copilot`, the Andrea drawer in `public/app.js`).
